# Investigate model and effort routing feasibility

## Task Overview

Investigate issue 463 before selecting an architecture. Produce the source-backed report at `.dev/tasks/to-do/TASK-RESEARCH-model-effort-router-20261109-023552/RESEARCH-REPORT-model-effort-router.md`, covering launch/session selection, per-request routing, classifier overhead and privacy, effort/provider contracts, actual local callers and test assertions, the supplied public router, alternatives and unresolved Jev identity. This is a research task, not implementation, deployment, benchmarking or permission to change product defaults.

The preparation gate accepted scope sufficiency for constructing this task only. Deeper local research, external investigations, synthesis, report validation, source fidelity and exhaustive anti-omission remain unperformed and must pass independently. The builder cannot certify those gates or execute this task.

## Key Objectives

1. Produce five deeper local investigations, three gap-driven external investigations, four synthesis files, a gap log and one ten-section final report, preserving all intermediates.
2. Preserve explicit user model/tier/effort choices and issue 462's user-configured Coder-role default; Tier1b is researcher execution policy, not a product default.
3. Compare source-backed options, including current policy/no new router, without inventing Jev identity, deployed policy, provider compatibility, performance, price or quality evidence.
4. Complete independent, report-only review waves, serialized blocking fixes, independent verification, source fidelity and exhaustive meaning-based anti-omission before claiming completion.

Exact ten-section format: (1) Problem Statement with research question, why and trigger; (2) Current State Analysis with actual subsystem details and path/line evidence for every claim, summary table and supported ASCII flow; (3) Target State with desired behavior, testable success criteria and constraints; (4) Gap Analysis table Gap | Current State | Target State | Severity | Notes, genuine unwired in-scope shipped/enabled promised capability is Critical/High defect linked to real remediation task absent cited exclusion, request coordinator if extra task write exceeds authority, never invent promised capability or normalize defect as future work; (5) External Research Findings topic subsections Finding/Source URL/Relevance HIGH-MEDIUM-LOW/Relationship supports-extends-contradicts plus external summary; (6) Options Analysis 2+ distinct options, each Description/How it works and Aspect | Assessment with Effort XS-S-M-L-XL, Risk Low-Medium-High, Reuse of existing code, Files/systems affected, Pros, Cons, comparison Criterion | actual option names for effort/risk/maintainability/integration complexity/reuse plus task-specific criteria; (7) Recommendation naming option and rationale referencing comparison/tradeoffs; (8) CONDITIONAL Implementation Plan, named phase goal/dependencies, Step | Action | Files | Details, integration checklist with actual file/function boundaries and no execution authority; (9) Open Questions table # | Question | Impact | Suggested Resolution; (10) Evidence Trail Codebase File | Topic | Agent Type and Web File | Topic, every synthesis artifact and gap log, all actual relied-on additions. Assembly Process: header first, read components in order and place sections incrementally, generate Table of Contents from ACTUAL headings only after placement, cross-check gaps4 have steps8 or unresolved questions9, options6 cite state2/external5, recommendations7 follow comparison6, questions9 not already answered elsewhere and evidence10 lists EVERY research/synth file.
